cosign.rs: parses cosign bundle JSON {mediaType, verificationMaterial:{x509CertificateChain, tlogEntries}, dsseEnvelope}; verifies ECDSA-P256 or Ed25519 signature over DSSE payload (p256/ed25519-dalek; sigstore-rs per ADR-012); extracts leaf cert SAN (Fulcio identity URI, e.g. https://github.com/...), validates chain against embedded Fulcio root CA (certs/fulcio_roots.pem); CosignBundle{payload_hash:[u8;32], signature:Vec<u8>, leaf_cert:X509Cert, log_id:String, integrated_time:i64}. rekor.rs: GET https://rekor.sigstore.dev/api/v1/log/entries/{uuid} (configurable base URL); parses LogEntry{body:base64(Rekord), integratedTime, logID, logIndex, verification:{signedEntryTimestamp, inclusionProof}}; verifies inclusion proof by reconstructing Merkle root from proof.hashes+leaf hash (SHA256), compares against signed tree head fetched from GET /api/v1/log and signature-verified with embedded Rekor public key (certs/rekor.pub, ECDSA P-256); RekorVerifier::verify(log_id) -> Result<RekorEntry>, O(log N) Merkle ops. fulcio.rs: FulcioVerifier::verify_chain(chain) -> Result<Identity> — WebPKI chain validation (webpki crate) against Fulcio root + intermediate CAs; strict: reject certs expired at integrated_time, check CodeSigning EKU, extract SAN URI as Identity{issuer, subject}. intoto.rs: parses DSSE envelope {payload:base64, payloadType, signatures:[{sig,keyid}]} (in-toto crate / custom DSSE parser per ADR-013, P2); decodes payload as in_toto::Statement{_type, subject, predicateType, predicate}; supports predicateType = "https://slsa.dev/provenance/v0.2" and v1.0; InTotoStatement → SlsaPredicate{builder:{id}, buildType, metadata:{buildInvocationId, completeness}, materials:Vec<{uri,digest}>}. slsa.rs: SlsaLevel::determine(pred, cert_identity) -> u8 — Level 0: no attestation; Level 1: SLSA provenance present (predicate parseable); Level 2: hosted build + signed attestation (Fulcio cert SAN matches builder URI); Level 3: hardened builder (builder ID in SLSA L3 registry slsa_builders.json, embedded via include_str!). hash_pin.rs: verify_hashes(component, artifact_bytes) -> Result<()> computes SHA-256/SHA-512 over bytes, compares with component.hashes, fails on first mismatch → ScsError::HashMismatch{algorithm, expected, actual}; artifact bytes sourced from registry manifest digest fetch (OCI) or lockfile-recorded hash (npm SRI, Cargo checksum.json) — never re-download full artifact, use digest-fetch API. cache.rs: sled tree provenance_cache/{purl_sha256} → serialized ProvenanceResult, TTL 24h in metadata; Cache::get_or_verify(purl, verifier) -> ProvenanceResult reduces Rekor API calls on repeated scans. Concurrency: verify_component is async (tokio); components verified with FuturesUnordered, max 8 concurrent Rekor/Fulcio connections (semaphore). Observability: span per component tracing::info_span!("provenance_verify", purl=%c.purl); metrics scs_prov_verified_total, scs_prov_failed_total, scs_prov_cache_hit_total, scs_rekor_latency_ms histogram.

7.3 Deliverable

  • scscli scan --verify-provenance runs full Sigstore/Rekor/in-toto verification for all components that have cosign bundle metadata in registry OCI annotations.
  • Each Component in JSON output carries provenance: { slsa_level, verified, rekor_log_id, failure_reason }.
  • SARIF result.properties.provenance_verified, slsa_level fields emitted.
  • scs-bench fixture rekor_mock_server spins up local Rekor-compatible HTTP stub; provenance tests run offline.
  • Operators inspect: scscli scan --verify-provenance --json | jq '[.components[] | select(.provenance.verified == false)]'.

7.4 Acceptance Criteria

Check Target
ECDSA-P256 signature verify 50 real cosign bundles pass; 5 tampered bundles rejected
Merkle inclusion proof 10 log entries verify against embedded Rekor pubkey
Fulcio chain validation 20 valid cert chains pass; 3 expired/wrong-EKU rejected
SLSA level determination L0/L1/L2/L3 classification correct for 20 fixture attestations
Hash pinning 10 component fixtures: 8 match, 2 mismatch → HashMismatch error
Provenance cache Second run with same purl → cache hit; zero Rekor calls
Concurrency cap 8-worker semaphore tested: no deadlock under 100 components
Rekor latency p95 < 2 s per component (mock server); real-world advisory < 5 s
Fail-closed invariant Corrupt bundle → verified: false, never true

7.5 Conformance Fixtures

File Purpose Expected output
fixtures/prov/cosign_bundle_valid.json Real cosign bundle from gcr.io verified: true, slsa_level >= 2
fixtures/prov/cosign_bundle_tampered_sig.json Signature bytes mutated verified: false, failure_reason: "signature_invalid"
fixtures/prov/cosign_bundle_expired_cert.json Cert expired before integrated_time verified: false, failure_reason: "cert_expired"
fixtures/prov/rekor_entry_valid.json Real Rekor log entry + inclusion proof Merkle proof verifies
fixtures/prov/rekor_entry_bad_proof.json Hashes truncated RekorError::InclusionProofInvalid
fixtures/prov/intoto_slsa_v02.json SLSA v0.2 predicate slsa_level = 2
fixtures/prov/intoto_slsa_v10.json SLSA v1.0 predicate slsa_level = 3 if builder in registry
fixtures/prov/intoto_bad_dsse.json Malformed base64 payload InTotoError::DsseDecode
fixtures/prov/hash_mismatch.json Component with wrong SHA-256 HashMismatch finding
fixtures/prov/hash_match.json Component with correct SHA-256 no finding
Replay: all fixtures checksummed in fixtures/prov/SHA256SUMS; CI verifies before test run.

7.6 Pitfalls

  1. Rekor STH clock skew — integrated_time in log entry vs local clock can differ; do not validate against SystemTime::now() — use cert's notBefore/notAfter window bracketed by integrated_time. Manifests as spurious cert-expired errors. Debug: RUST_LOG=scs_provenance=debug prints cert validity window.
  2. Fulcio root CA rotation — Sigstore rotates roots periodically; hard-coded PEM bundle becomes stale → all chains fail. Mitigation: bundle from TUF metadata; add --sigstore-tuf-root flag (future); for now, alert on 100% chain failure rate spike (scs_prov_chain_fail_total > threshold).
  3. OCI manifest digest vs layer digest confusion — Component.hashes may record layer digest (sha256 of compressed layer), not manifest digest; hash pinning against manifest digest will always mismatch. Distinguish via OCI media type: application/vnd.oci.image.manifest.v1+json → manifest digest; document which digest type each ecosystem provides.
  4. in-toto predicate version mismatch — predicateType string check is strict; an unknown predicate version (e.g. v0.3) → slsa_level = 1 (attestation present but not understood); log warning; do not fail verification.
  5. Rekor rate limiting — public Rekor instance throttles at ~10 req/s; 100-component scan → 10 s sequential; mitigated by FuturesUnordered concurrency cap + cache. Cache miss storm on first scan of large repo. Pre-warm cache with scscli prov cache-fill.
  6. Bundle not present — most components lack cosign bundle (e.g., PyPI sdist, old npm packages); provenance: null is correct; no error; document in SARIF as slsa_level: 0.
  7. DSSE vs JWT envelope — older in-toto implementations use JWT (JWS Compact); intoto.rs must detect and parse both. JWT: header.payload.signature base64url; DSSE: JSON {payload, payloadType, signatures}.

7.7 Performance Concerns

  • Rekor HTTP round-trip dominates: 100 components × 500 ms = 50 s sequential; async with 8-way concurrency → ~6 s; further reduced by cache hits on re-scans.
  • Merkle proof computation: O(log N) SHA256 hashes; N ≈ 10^8 entries → ≈ 26 hashes; < 1 µs; not a bottleneck.
  • Certificate chain parse (webpki): ~200 µs per chain; 100 chains = 20 ms; acceptable.
  • Sled cache lookup: ~10 µs per key; 100 lookups = 1 ms; negligible.
  • Memory: cosign bundle JSON ≈ 4 KB; 100 in-flight = 400 KB; well within limits.
  • DSSE base64 decode: single allocation per bundle; use base64::Engine::decode into pre-allocated Vec<u8>; avoid intermediate String.
  • TLS handshake: reuse reqwest::Client (connection pool) across all Rekor/Fulcio calls; do not create new client per component.

7.8 Edge Cases

  • Component with multiple hashes (SHA-256 + SHA-512) — verify all; fail if any mismatches; emit all failures.
  • Rekor entry references different artifact — log entry artifact hash != Component.hashes[sha256] → RekorError::ArtifactMismatch; not a chain failure, a correlation failure.
  • Concurrent verify + cache write — sled write is atomic per key; get_or_insert pattern using compare_and_swap to prevent double-fetch race.
  • Rekor network timeout (5 s) — tokio::time::timeout(5s, rekor_fetch) → on timeout emit verified: false, failure_reason: "rekor_timeout"; do not retry inline (cache miss → retry on next scan).
  • Attestation with empty materials — valid SLSA v1 with no input materials → level capped at 2 (builder signed but no provenance trace); document.
  • SHA-1 hash in lockfile — accept for matching but log WARN: sha1_only purl={}; SHA-1 is deprecated for integrity; findings note weak_hash flag.
  • Null rekor_log_id — component signed but not logged; rekor_inclusion: false; slsa_level capped at 1.

7.9 Failure Analysis

Failure Detection Blast radius Containment Recovery
Fulcio root CA expired 100% chain-fail spike All provenance unverified Emit warn; continue scan; report verified: false Update embedded PEM bundle; hotfix deploy
Rekor unreachable HTTP timeout, scs_rekor_timeout_total Provenance pass produces all verified: false Serve from cache; scan continues Re-run with --skip-rekor flag (policy permitting)
Hash mismatch on known good component HashMismatch finding generated Single component flagged Analyst review; may indicate supply chain tamper Freeze dep version; incident escalation
DSSE parse panic intoto_parse_errors_total spike Component gets slsa_level=0 Result-gated; no panic propagation Fix parser; re-scan
Cache corruption (sled) CacheError on get Forced live Rekor fetch Auto-fallback to live fetch; log error Delete cache dir; rebuild on next scan
Postmortem signals: scs_prov_failed_total / scs_prov_verified_total > 0.5 triggers alert; investigate Fulcio CA bundle staleness first.

7.10 Operational Notes

  • Metrics: scs_prov_verified_total{ecosystem}, scs_prov_failed_total{reason}, scs_prov_cache_hit_total, scs_rekor_latency_ms{p50,p95,p99}, scs_slsa_level{level=0..3} distribution.
  • Tracing: per-component span provenance_verify with fields purl, slsa_level, cache_hit, rekor_log_id.
  • Logging: INFO on verified; WARN on slsa_level=0 or rekor_inclusion=false; ERROR on HashMismatch.
  • Config: [provenance] section in scs.toml: rekor_url, fulcio_roots_pem, concurrency = 8, cache_ttl_hours = 24, skip_ecosystems = [].
  • Deployment: embedded CA PEM bundles require rebuild on rotation; consider TUF-based dynamic trust root (Phase 11+).
  • CI integration: --verify-provenance --slsa-level-min=2 gates on SLSA Level < 2; report includes per-component level in SARIF annotations.
  • Rollback: --skip-provenance disables pass entirely; no schema change (fields remain null); backward-compatible.

20. Phase 8 — Malicious-package detection — typosquatting (name edit-distance vs popular), dependency confusion, suspicious install scripts, entropy/obfuscation, maintainer/age signals

8.1 Objectives

  • Implement scs-threat with five distinct detectors: (1) typosquatting via edit-distance against a curated popular-package corpus, (2) dependency confusion detection (internal-named package resolvable from public registry), (3) suspicious install-script heuristics (lifecycle hooks containing shell exec, curl, base64, obfuscated payloads), (4) payload entropy + identifier obfuscation scoring, (5) maintainer account age + download-velocity anomalies.
  • Each detector emits ThreatFinding { kind: ThreatKind, component: Purl, confidence: f32, detail: String } where confidence ∈ [0.0, 1.0]; ThreatKind enum: Typosquatting, DependencyConfusion, SuspiciousInstallScript, HighEntropyPayload, MaintainerAnomaly.
  • Detectors are independent; all run per component; results merged into Finding via threat: Vec<ThreatFinding>.
  • Constraints: detectors are static (no package download/install); install-script analysis uses manifest metadata only (package.json scripts.postinstall); entropy computed over script text in manifest; no runtime execution.
  • Scope: scs-threat, scs-core (new types), scs-report, scs-cli (--threat-scan flag, default enabled).

8.2 Detailed Implementation Steps

scs-core additions: ThreatKind enum + ThreatFinding struct as above; Finding.threats: Vec<ThreatFinding> parallel to advisory-based findings — threat findings always accompany a component purl even without an advisory. scs-threat crate structure:

scs-threat/src/
  lib.rs           — ThreatScanner: run_all(components: &[Component]) -> Vec<ThreatFinding>
  typosquat.rs     — edit-distance detector; popular corpus; BK-tree index
  dep_confusion.rs — internal namespace detection; public registry probe
  install_script.rs — postinstall/preinstall script heuristics
  entropy.rs       — Shannon entropy + identifier entropy scoring
  maintainer.rs    — maintainer age + velocity signals (registry metadata)
  corpus.rs        — popular package list loader; BK-tree construction

typosquat.rs: embedded corpus data/popular_packages_{ecosystem}.json, curated top-5000/ecosystem (npm, PyPI, crates.io, Maven, RubyGems) from registry download stats (quarterly update); BK-tree (bk-tree crate) keyed by Damerau-Levenshtein distance, query threshold=2 edits (configurable); TyposquatDetector::check(name, ecosystem) -> Vec<(String,u32)> (matched popular names + distance); also checks character-substitution patterns (0→o, 1→l, rn→m, - vs _) and homoglyph table data/homoglyphs.json; confidence: distance=1→0.9, distance=2→0.6, homoglyph match→0.85; emits ThreatFinding{kind:Typosquatting, detail:"similar to {popular}: dist={d}"}. dep_confusion.rs: scope npm @scope/pkg, PyPI com.company.pkg-style, Go module path with private prefix; config [threat.dep_confusion] internal_prefixes=["@myorg/","com.mycompany."] in scs.toml; component name matches internal prefix → check resolvability from public registry via pre-fetched metadata cache (no live HTTP); found on public registry → ThreatFinding{kind:DependencyConfusion, confidence:0.95}; Birsan-2021 pattern (P1) (internal name registered publicly at higher version → resolver picks public) surfaced here, actual version comparison uses scs-resolve graph. install_script.rs: input Component.metadata: HashMap<String,Value> populated by scs-manifest for npm (scripts.postinstall/preinstall/install); heuristic rules add to raw_score (threshold 0.5 → finding): curl|wget|fetch +0.4, sh -c/exec/eval +0.3, base64 -d/atob +0.5, process.env + network call pattern +0.3, script length >500 chars +0.1, chmod +x/sudo +0.2; InstallScriptAnalyzer::score(script) -> f32 sums matched weights clamped to 1.0; confidence=raw_score, detail=matched rule names. entropy.rs: shannon_entropy(s) -> f32 standard formula over char frequencies, >4.5 bits/char in install scripts suggests base64/encrypted payloads; identifier_entropy(script) -> f32 tokenizes by non-alphanum, avg identifier length <3 + entropy >4.0 → obfuscation signal; combined score >0.7 → ThreatFinding{kind:HighEntropyPayload}; applied to install-script text, README (if fetched from SBOM), inline binary blobs in manifest. maintainer.rs: input Component.metadata fields maintainer_created_at:DateTime<Utc>, weekly_downloads:u64, version_published_at:DateTime<Utc>, maintainer_count:u8; signals: account age <7 days at publish → confidence+=0.5; download velocity 0/week for 6+ months then spike >10× → +0.3; single maintainer/no GitHub link/no verified email → +0.2; version published within 24h of account creation → +0.4; MaintainerAnalyzer::score(meta) -> f32 sums clamped 1.0; data sourced from OSV advisory metadata where available or npm registry API cached during manifest parse. ThreatScanner orchestration: run_all iterates components, runs detectors in parallel via rayon (CPU-bound, no I/O in detectors), collects Vec<ThreatFinding>; Rayon thread pool, no async; ecosystem corpus loaded once at scanner init, Arc<Corpus> shared across threads. Observability: counters scs_threat_typosquat_total, scs_threat_dep_confusion_total, scs_threat_install_script_total, scs_threat_entropy_total, scs_threat_maintainer_total; span threat_scan covering all detectors, per-component child spans at DEBUG.

8.3 Deliverable

  • scscli scan --threat-scan (default on) emits threats array per component in JSON + SARIF.
  • Typosquatting detector queries BK-tree for all 5 ecosystems; distance-2 matches flagged.
  • Dependency confusion detector reads internal_prefixes from scs.toml; flags matching components found on public registry metadata.
  • Install-script heuristic scores npm postinstall fields from parsed package.json components.
  • Entropy scoring applied to all install-script text present in manifest metadata.
  • Maintainer anomaly flags surfaced when registry metadata available.
  • scs-bench fixture: 1000-component scan with 50 planted threats → all 50 detected in < 500 ms.

8.4 Acceptance Criteria

Check Target
Typosquat BK-tree build (npm, 5000 names) < 100 ms; query < 1 ms per name
Typosquat fixture (30 known squats) >= 28/30 detected (recall ≥ 93%); false-positive rate < 5% on 500 legit names
Dep confusion fixture 10/10 internal-prefix matches flagged; 0/10 false positives on public-only names
Install script rules 20 malicious fixtures: 18/20 flagged; 10 benign fixtures: 0 flagged
Entropy: event-stream backdoor script Entropy > 4.5 → HighEntropyPayload finding
Maintainer age < 7d fixture MaintainerAnomaly emitted with confidence >= 0.5
Rayon parallel scan (1000 components) < 500 ms wall on 4-core
Zero false negatives on planted threats 50/50 planted → 50 findings

8.5 Conformance Fixtures

File Purpose Expected output
fixtures/threat/typosquat_positives.json 30 known typosquats vs popular corpus >= 28 flagged
fixtures/threat/typosquat_negatives.json 500 legitimate package names <= 25 false positives
fixtures/threat/dep_confusion_internal.json 10 internal-scoped packages on public registry 10/10 flagged
fixtures/threat/dep_confusion_safe.json 10 legitimate public packages 0 flagged
fixtures/threat/install_malicious.json 20 npm packages with malicious postinstall >= 18 flagged
fixtures/threat/install_benign.json 10 npm packages with benign scripts (tsc, webpack) 0 flagged
fixtures/threat/event_stream_backdoor.js Actual event-stream backdoor script text entropy > 4.5
fixtures/threat/maintainer_new_account.json Account created 3 days before publish MaintainerAnomaly, confidence >= 0.5
fixtures/threat/xz_utils_metadata.json xz-utils 2024 compromise signals At least maintainer anomaly flagged
fixtures/threat/planted_50.json 1000 components; 50 planted threats 50/50 detected
Regression: fixtures/threat/SHA256SUMS pins all fixtures; golden ThreatFinding arrays stored as JSON; CI diffs on change.

8.6 Pitfalls

  1. BK-tree false positives on short names — packages with 2-3 char names (e.g., fs, os, io) match almost everything within distance 2; add minimum name length 4 to corpus membership; distance threshold 1 for names ≤ 6 chars. Manifests as high false-positive rate on short names; debug by inspecting scs_threat_typosquat_fps_total.
  2. Dep-confusion false positive on forks — a legitimately dual-published package (internal + public) is flagged; mitigate with dep_confusion.allowlist in scs.toml; document pattern; confidence = 0.95 not 1.0 to allow policy waiver.
  3. Install-script heuristics on build tools — postinstall: "node-gyp build" triggers exec rule; node-gyp is common + legitimate; add ecosystem-specific allowlist (data/install_script_allowlist.json); not hardcoded — updateable. Debug: scscli scan --debug-install-scripts prints rule hits.
  4. Entropy high for minified JS — legitimate uglifyjs-minified scripts have entropy > 4.5; disambiguate via install-script rule score: if entropy high but no network/exec rules → confidence = 0.3 (flag, low severity). Require combined score threshold.
  5. Maintainer metadata absent — most ecosystems other than npm don't expose maintainer account age; MaintainerAnalyzer must gracefully return confidence = 0.0 + skip (not error). Signal: scs_threat_maintainer_metadata_missing_total counter.
  6. BK-tree corpus staleness — top-5000 list becomes stale; new popular packages added, old ones removed; stale corpus = missed new squats. Mitigation: embed corpus version + date; --update-corpus flag refreshes from registry stats API (future); CI fails if corpus > 180 days old (corpus_age_days metric).
  7. Unicode homoglyphs in package names — сurl (Cyrillic с) vs curl; standard Damerau-Levenshtein is byte-based and misses Unicode substitutions; normalize names to NFKC before BK-tree insert + query; flag names containing non-ASCII after normalization as additional signal (confidence += 0.2).

8.7 Performance Concerns

  • BK-tree query: O(log N) expected per name, N = 5000; batch all component names before scan using Rayon; avoid per-component tree rebuild.
  • Corpus loading: 5 × 5000 = 25 k entries × ~20 bytes = 500 KB; load once at ThreatScanner::new(), wrap in Arc; shared read with no lock needed.
  • Entropy computation: O(|script|) per script; scripts rarely > 10 KB; no allocation needed (histogram on stack [u32; 128]).
  • Install-script regex: precompile all regexes at scanner init using lazy_static/once_cell; single-pass multi-pattern match via aho-corasick crate for throughput.
  • Rayon parallelism: rayon::par_iter over components; each detector is pure function → no shared mutable state; detectors run per-component in parallel.
  • Registry metadata cache: maintainer data fetched from scs-manifest pre-cache; scs-threat reads from Component.metadata — no additional I/O during threat scan.
  • Memory: 1000 components × 5 detectors × avg 200-byte ThreatFinding = ~1 MB; negligible.

8.8 Edge Cases

  • Package name with path separators (../evil) — normalize name: strip /, ., \ before BK-tree lookup; log warning; do not panic.
  • Empty install script — score = 0.0; no finding; not an error.
  • Install script is a file path ("postinstall": "./scripts/setup.sh") — path reference, not inline script; flag as InstallScriptExternal (cannot analyze); confidence = 0.1 to surface for review.
  • Multiple maintainers, one new account — score only on new-account maintainer; do not penalize package for co-maintainers; take max confidence across maintainers.
  • Component with no ecosystem — typosquat + dep confusion skip (corpus is per-ecosystem); other detectors still run.
  • Homoglyph in corpus name itself — normalize corpus entries to NFKC on load; reject non-normalized entries with warning.
  • Zero-download package — no velocity anomaly possible; skip velocity signal; do not divide by zero in velocity ratio.
  • Rayon panic in detector — wrap each detector call in std::panic::catch_unwind; return empty Vec<ThreatFinding> on panic + log error; do not abort scan.

8.9 Failure Analysis

Failure Detection Blast radius Containment Recovery
BK-tree OOM on huge corpus RSS spike, OOM kill Threat scan aborted Cap corpus at 10 k per ecosystem; log corpus_truncated Reduce corpus; rebuild
Corpus file missing/corrupt serde_json::Error at init All typosquat + dep_confusion detectors disabled Log ERROR: corpus_load_failed; continue with detectors disabled Restore from embedded fallback
Rayon thread panic catch_unwind catches; threat_detector_panic_total counter Single component's threat results empty Log purl + panic message Fix detector bug; redeploy
False positive storm (rule too aggressive) FP rate metric spike; user reports Noise in findings; policy over-gates Lower rule weight or add allowlist entry Hot-patch scs.toml allowlist; no rebuild
Registry metadata absent (ecosystem gap) maintainer_metadata_missing_total Maintainer detector produces no signal Document per-ecosystem coverage; silence metric Future: add per-ecosystem metadata fetch
Postmortem: if typosquat FP rate > 10% (measured via user feedback loop), audit corpus minimum-length filter and distance threshold; consider raising threshold to 1 edit for names ≤ 8 chars.

8.10 Operational Notes

  • Metrics: scs_threat_{kind}_total{ecosystem}, scs_threat_corpus_age_days, scs_threat_scan_duration_ms, scs_threat_detector_panic_total, scs_threat_typosquat_fps_total (manual feedback).
  • Tracing: INFO span threat_scan{component_count} with child spans per detector at DEBUG; log per-finding at WARN.
  • Logging: WARN per finding with kind, purl, confidence, detail; INFO on clean scan; ERROR on corpus load failure.
  • Config (scs.toml [threat] section): enabled = true, typosquat_threshold = 2, entropy_threshold = 4.5, install_score_threshold = 0.5, dep_confusion.internal_prefixes = [], dep_confusion.allowlist = [], install_script.allowlist_patterns = [].
  • Corpus updates: scscli threat update-corpus --ecosystem npm (future CLI command); stores updated JSON to $SCS_DATA_DIR/corpus/; scanner prefers data-dir corpus over embedded.
  • CI integration: --fail-on-threat confidence=0.9 exits non-zero if any ThreatFinding.confidence >= 0.9; configurable threshold per kind in policy.
  • Rollback: [threat] enabled = false disables entire pass; no schema change; findings array becomes empty.
  • Compatibility: ThreatFinding is additive to Finding JSON; old consumers ignore threats field; SARIF properties.threat_kind added per result.

21. Phase 9 — License + policy compliance — SPDX license-expression parsing, allow/deny lists, copyleft detection, policy DSL, VEX consumption, waivers/ignores

9.1 Objectives

  • SPDX expression parser: full SPDX-2.3 expression grammar (AND, OR, WITH, +, LicenseRef-) → AST; normalize aliases (GPL-2.0 → GPL-2.0-only).
  • Copyleft detector: classify licenses → Permissive | WeakCopyleft | StrongCopyleft | Proprietary | Unknown; flag transitive contamination paths.
  • Policy DSL: TOML/YAML policy files with allow/deny license sets, severity thresholds, scope filters (prod/dev), ecosystem-specific overrides, waiver blocks.
  • VEX consumer: ingest OpenVEX + CycloneDX VEX documents; update Finding.vex_status (NotAffected, Fixed, UnderInvestigation, Affected) before gate decision.
  • Waivers/ignores: time-bounded, per-advisory or per-component waivers with mandatory justification; expired waivers escalate to failures.
  • Gate output: boolean pass | fail with structured reason list; deterministic ordering; zero false-negatives on deny rules.
  • Scope: entirely within scs-policy; reads DependencyGraph, Vec<Finding>, Sbom, Vec<Provenance>; emits PolicyResult.
  • Constraints: policy evaluation is pure (no I/O, no network); all VEX documents pre-loaded before evaluation; MSRV 1.78.

9.2 Detailed Implementation Steps

scs-policy/src/spdx.rs — EBNF grammar:

expr     ::= and-expr ( "OR" and-expr )*
and-expr ::= primary ( "AND" primary )*
primary  ::= id-expr | "(" expr ")"
id-expr  ::= license-id ["+"] | "LicenseRef-" ref | "NOASSERTION" | "NONE"
with-expr::= id-expr "WITH" exception-id

parse_spdx_expression(s) -> Result<SpdxExpr, SpdxError> recursive-descent O(n); SpdxExpr enum Single(SpdxId), And(Box<SpdxExpr>,Box<SpdxExpr>), Or(...), With(SpdxId,SpdxException); SpdxId::normalize() → canonical SPDX 3.x id via alias table (GPL-2.0→GPL-2.0-only, Apache-2.0 stable, etc.); SpdxExpr::satisfies(allowed) -> bool (OR: any branch satisfies, AND: all branches satisfy); SpdxExpr::contains_copyleft() -> CopyleftLevel traverses AST, worst-level wins. scs-policy/src/classify.rs — CopyleftLevel: Permissive|WeakCopyleft|StrongCopyleft|NetworkCopyleft|Proprietary|Unknown; static table keyed by SpdxId → CopyleftLevel covering SPDX 3.25 license list; classify(expr) -> CopyleftLevel: AND→max(left,right), OR→min(left,right) (conservative: best case for OR); transitive contamination walks DependencyGraph from prod-scope roots, any strong-copyleft transitive dep → PolicyViolation::CopyleftContamination{path}. scs-policy/src/policy.rs — Policy struct deserialized from .scs/policy.toml:

[policy]
severity_threshold = "High"       # block on High+
allow_licenses = ["MIT", "Apache-2.0", "BSD-*"]
deny_licenses  = ["GPL-3.0-only", "AGPL-*"]
deny_advisories = ["CVE-2021-44228"]
scopes = ["Prod"]                 # only gate on prod deps
[[waivers]]
  advisory = "GHSA-xxxx-xxxx"
  expires  = "2026-12-31"
  reason   = "mitigated by WAF rule #1234"

PolicyEngine::new(policy, vex_docs) -> PolicyEngine; PolicyEngine::evaluate(graph, findings, sbom) -> PolicyResult; PolicyResult{pass:bool, violations:Vec<PolicyViolation>, applied_waivers:Vec<WaiverApplication>, vex_applied:Vec<VexApplication>}; PolicyViolation variants SeverityThreshold, DeniedLicense, CopyleftContamination, DeniedAdvisory, ExpiredWaiver, UnverifiedProvenance. scs-policy/src/vex.rs — VexDocument parsed from OpenVEX JSON (schema v0.2, P12/S11) or CycloneDX VEX XML/JSON; VexStatement{product_purl, vuln_id(CVE/GHSA/OSV), status:VexStatus, justification, timestamp}; VexStatus: NotAffected|Affected|Fixed|UnderInvestigation; apply_vex(findings, vex) matches on (purl, vuln_id), updates Finding.vex_status, only NotAffected+Fixed suppress gate failure; VEX timestamp ordering: newest statement wins per (product,vuln) pair; provenance of VEX doc (source URL, hash) logged for auditability. scs-policy/src/waiver.rs — Waiver{advisory:Option<AdvisoryId>, component:Option<Purl>, expires:Option<Date>, reason:String}; WaiverEngine::check(finding, now) -> WaiverOutcome: Applied|Expired|NotMatched; expired waivers → PolicyViolation::ExpiredWaiver logged with original reason + expiry date; applies_to: advisory glob match (GHSA-* prefix), exact purl match, or component name glob. Observability: tracing::info! per policy decision; tracing::warn! for expired waiver; tracing::debug! for each SPDX parse; counters policy.violations.total, policy.vex_applied.total, policy.waivers.expired. Test harness (scs-policy/tests/): spdx_parse.rs (200+ expression strings incl. malformed, round-trip parse→display); policy_eval.rs (fixture-driven: policy TOML + findings JSON → assert PolicyResult); vex_apply.rs (OpenVEX fixture → assert vex_status propagation); waiver_expiry.rs (mock clock; expired vs active waivers).

9.3 Deliverable

  • PolicyEngine::evaluate compiles, passes all tests, integrates into scs-cli scan pipeline.
  • scs-cli scan --policy .scs/policy.toml exits non-zero on violations; --vex doc.vex.json accepted.
  • scs-report consumes PolicyResult to annotate findings with [WAIVED], [VEX: NotAffected], [BLOCKED].
  • Operators can cat .scs/policy.toml to audit all active rules; waiver list in report shows expiry dates.

9.4 Acceptance Criteria

Check Target
SPDX parse fuzz 0 panics on 100 k random strings; graceful SpdxError
Known-bad expressions "GPL-3.0-only AND MIT" correctly classified StrongCopyleft
Policy gate deny_licenses = ["AGPL-3.0-only"] + component with that license → pass=false
VEX suppression NotAffected VEX on CVE-2021-44228 → finding not gating; still reported with status
Waiver expiry waiver with expires = "2020-01-01" → ExpiredWaiver violation (not suppressed)
Scope filter dev-scope dep with GPL → no violation if scopes = ["Prod"]
Evaluation latency ≤ 50 ms for 10 000 findings + 1 000-node graph on CI hardware
Policy TOML round-trip serialize + deserialize preserves all fields; no lossy fields
Zero false-negatives every deny_licenses match in graph surfaces as violation

9.5 Conformance Fixtures

Fixture Purpose Expected output
fixtures/policy/spdx_expressions.txt 200+ SPDX strings, one per line, annotated valid/invalid parse success/error per line
fixtures/policy/policy_basic.toml MIT/Apache allow, GPL deny, severity=High violations on GPL + CVSS≥7
fixtures/policy/policy_scoped.toml prod-only scope dev GPL dep not flagged
fixtures/policy/vex_not_affected.json OpenVEX NotAffected for CVE-2021-44228 finding.vex_status = NotAffected
fixtures/policy/vex_cyclonedx.xml CycloneDX VEX format same outcome as JSON VEX
fixtures/policy/waiver_expired.toml waiver expires 2020-01-01 ExpiredWaiver violation
fixtures/policy/waiver_active.toml waiver expires 2099-01-01 violation suppressed; applied_waivers populated
fixtures/policy/malformed_policy.toml invalid severity_threshold value PolicyLoadError with field name
fixtures/policy/copyleft_transitive.json graph: root→A→B(GPL-3.0) CopyleftContamination with path [B→A→root]
fixtures/policy/licenseref.txt LicenseRef-Custom AND MIT classified Unknown (not in SPDX list)

9.6 Pitfalls

Pitfall Manifestation Root cause Debug signal Recovery
GPL-2.0 alias confusion GPL-2.0 not matched by deny_licenses = ["GPL-2.0-only"] SPDX-2 vs SPDX-3 id rename; normalization missing RUST_LOG=debug shows raw id pre-normalize add alias table; normalize before matching
VEX timestamp skew newer Affected overridden by older NotAffected VEX statements not sorted by timestamp before last-wins vex_apply debug log shows selected statement sort by timestamp desc before dedup
Waiver glob over-match GHSA-* waiver suppresses all GHSA advisories glob too broad; author intent was one advisory waiver audit log shows count of matches require exact id for waivers; warn on glob matching >1
OR-expression copyleft undercount AGPL-3.0 OR MIT classified Permissive OR → min(levels) = Permissive; but AGPL present classify debug trace shows OR path taken log warning when OR branch contains StrongCopyleft
LicenseRef unknown silently passes LicenseRef-Proprietary not in SPDX list → Unknown; allow-list check skips Unknown allow-list only enumerates SPDX ids Unknown license warning in policy output policy option deny_unknown_licenses = true
Expired waiver not detected clock source is wall-clock; in offline/CI frozen time waiver check uses SystemTime::now() waiver expiry field in report shows stale inject now: Date parameter; override in tests
Policy file path resolution --policy relative path resolves against CWD, not manifest dir std::fs::canonicalize before parse IoError on wrong path shows resolved path canonicalize + error message shows attempted path

9.7 Performance Concerns

  • SPDX parse: O(n) per expression; typical <200 chars → negligible; bulk parse 50 k components < 100 ms.
  • License classification table: HashMap<SpdxId, CopyleftLevel> pre-built at startup; O(1) lookup.
  • Policy evaluation loop: O(F × R) findings × rules; memoize per (purl, license) pair; expected < 5 ms for 10 k findings.
  • VEX matching: index by vuln_id on load; O(F) lookup during apply; no repeated scans.
  • Waiver matching: linear scan over waiver list; lists typically < 100 items; negligible.
  • Allocation: PolicyResult pre-allocates violations with Vec::with_capacity(findings.len()) to avoid realloc on dense violation sets.
  • No lock contention: PolicyEngine::evaluate is fully single-threaded pure function; no Mutex needed.

9.8 Edge Cases

  • SPDX: NONE → component explicitly declares no license; policy can deny_unlicensed = true.
  • SPDX: NOASSERTION → unknown; treated as Unknown; option to deny.
  • Component with empty licenses vec → treat as NOASSERTION; warn.
  • VEX with no product_purl (applies to all products) → match all findings for that vuln; log over-broad VEX.
  • Waiver with no expires → permanent waiver; warn! on load (policy hygiene).
  • Duplicate advisory aliases (CVE + GHSA same vuln) → waiver on GHSA also suppresses CVE match via alias resolution in scs-core.
  • Policy TOML missing [policy] header → PolicyLoadError::MissingSection.
  • Severity threshold "None" → no severity-based blocking; only license/explicit-deny rules apply.
  • Concurrent policy files (multiple --policy flags) → merge: allow-lists union, deny-lists union, waivers concatenate, threshold = most restrictive.

9.9 Failure Analysis

What breaks Detection Blast radius Containment Recovery Operator visibility
Policy file corrupt/invalid TOML PolicyLoadError at startup Scan aborts before evaluation Return exit code 2 (config error) Fix TOML; re-run Error message with line/col
VEX document schema mismatch VexParseError on load VEX not applied; findings un-suppressed Log error; continue scan without VEX Update VEX format or parser Warning in report header
SPDX expression parse failure SpdxError per component That component's license treated as Unknown Count unknown-license findings Upstream fix manifest license field [SPDX-PARSE-ERROR] annotation
Alias table staleness GPL-2.0 not normalized → missed deny False-negative violation None (silent miss) Update alias table; cut release Audit log shows raw vs normalized id
Waiver DB load failure File not found All waivers missing → more violations Fail scan with config error Fix path; re-run IoError message

9.10 Operational Notes

  • Metrics (via metrics crate, Prometheus-compatible): scs_policy_violations_total{type="license|severity|copyleft|waiver_expired"} counter; scs_policy_vex_applied_total{status="NotAffected|Fixed|..."} counter; scs_policy_evaluation_duration_ms histogram.
  • Tracing spans: policy::evaluate (root), policy::vex_apply, policy::waiver_check, policy::license_classify.
  • Structured log fields: advisory_id, component_purl, license_expr, waiver_id, vex_source.
  • Policy versioning: policy TOML includes version = "1" field; future schema changes bump version; old files rejected with migration hint.
  • Audit trail: PolicyResult includes policy_hash: Sha256 of the policy file used; stored in scan report for reproducibility.
  • Rollback: policy file is external config; revert via git; no DB migration needed.
  • CI integration: --fail-on-warnings flag treats Unknown licenses and active-investigation VEX as failures.
  • Compatibility: scs-policy is a pure library; no network I/O; safe to call from WASM builds for browser-side preview.

22. Phase 10 — Reporting + CI integration — SARIF, JSON, table, SBOM diff, exit codes, PR annotations, baseline/delta scans

10.1 Objectives

  • SARIF 2.1.0: emit standards-compliant SARIF for GitHub/GitLab/ADO code-scanning; include result.locations (manifest file + line), rule definitions, ruleId → advisory id mapping.
  • JSON report: machine-readable ScanReport struct → pretty/compact JSON; include findings, policy result, provenance results, SBOM summary, feed snapshot id.
  • Table report: human-readable terminal table (Unicode box-drawing) with ANSI color; auto-detect TTY; --no-color flag.
  • SBOM diff: compare two SBOM snapshots; emit added/removed/version-changed components; used for PR delta reports.
  • Exit codes: 0 pass, 1 policy fail, 2 config/scan error, 3 advisory feed stale; composable in CI pipelines.
  • PR annotations: GitHub Actions ::error file=...:: + GitLab CI gl-sast-report.json annotation formats.
  • Baseline/delta scans: --baseline baseline.json flag; only report findings not in baseline (new findings); suppressed findings still counted.
  • Scope: scs-report crate; consumes ScanReport (aggregated from all prior phases); no scanning logic.

10.2 Detailed Implementation Steps

scs-report/src/sarif.rs — emit_sarif(report) -> SarifDocument serialized via serde_json, follows SARIF 2.1.0 JSON schema (oasis-open.org, S15): runs[0].tool.driver.rules[] one per advisory (id,name,shortDescription,helpUri OSV/NVD link,properties.severity); runs[0].results[] one per Finding (ruleId, level error/warning/note, message.text, locations[].physicalLocation manifest path+region.startLine if known, relatedLocations dep path steps); runs[0].properties.scsVersion/feedSnapshotId/scanTimestamp. Line-number annotation: scs-manifest stores SourceLocation{file,line,col} per component, propagated into Finding.location, used in SARIF region. SarifLevel mapping: CVSS Critical/High→error, Medium→warning, Low/Info→note, policy violation→error. scs-report/src/json_report.rs — ScanReport{scan_id:Uuid, timestamp, feed_snapshot_id, components_total, findings:Vec<Finding>, policy_result:PolicyResult, sbom_summary:SbomSummary, provenance_results:Vec<ProvenanceResult>, scan_duration_ms}; emit_json(report,pretty) -> String via serde_json::to_string[_pretty]; SbomSummary{components_total, components_with_vulns, licenses_histogram:HashMap<SpdxId,u32>, copyleft_count}; schema version field "scs_report_version":"1" for forward compatibility. scs-report/src/table.rs — comfy-table for Unicode box-drawing, columns Severity|Component|Advisory|Fixed|Status|Path; color via crossterm/termcolor, auto-detect is_terminal() (no ANSI if piped); summary row totals by severity + policy result (PASS green/FAIL red); --format table default when TTY, --format json/--format sarif explicit. scs-report/src/sbom_diff.rs — diff_sboms(base,head) -> SbomDiff; SbomDiff{added:Vec<Component>, removed:Vec<Component>, version_changed:Vec<(Component,Component)>, unchanged_count:u32}; keyed by PackageId (ecosystem+name), compares Version, O(n log n) sort-merge; diff emitted as sub-section in table/JSON/SARIF runs. scs-report/src/annotations.rs — emit_github_annotations(findings, policy) writes ::error file={path},line={n}::{message} to stdout; emit_gitlab_sast(report) -> String produces gl-sast-report.json schema (GitLab 15+); emit_ado_sarif(...) ADO code-scanning SARIF upload format (same as GitHub, endpoint differs); auto-detects CI environment via CI/GITHUB_ACTIONS/GITLAB_CI env vars. scs-report/src/baseline.rs — BaselineScan = ScanReport loaded from --baseline path (JSON); delta_findings(baseline, current) -> DeltaResult; DeltaResult{new_findings, resolved_findings, persisting_findings: Vec<Finding>}; match key (component.purl, advisory.id) stable across scans; --new-only flag gates exit code on new_findings only. Exit code table:

Code Meaning
0 Pass: no violations
1 Policy fail: violations found
2 Scan/config error: parse failure, feed unavailable
3 Advisory feed stale: snapshot older than --max-feed-age
4 Provenance failure: Sigstore/in-toto verification error
Test harness: sarif_schema.rs (emit SARIF, validate against embedded SARIF 2.1.0 JSON schema via jsonschema crate); json_roundtrip.rs (ScanReport serialize→deserialize→assert equality); sbom_diff.rs (fixture SBOM pair, assert SbomDiff fields); baseline.rs (baseline JSON + current findings → assert DeltaResult); exit_codes.rs (integration: scs-cli subprocess exit codes match policy result).

10.3 Deliverable

  • scs-cli scan --format sarif > results.sarif → valid SARIF 2.1.0 with findings + locations.
  • scs-cli scan --format json > report.json → machine-parseable; CI scripts can jq findings.
  • scs-cli scan --baseline baseline.json → exit 0 if no new findings vs. baseline.
  • GitHub Actions workflow can upload SARIF via github/codeql-action/upload-sarif@v3.
  • scs-cli diff-sbom base.spdx.json head.spdx.json → prints component diff table.

10.4 Acceptance Criteria

Check Target
SARIF schema validation 0 schema errors against SARIF 2.1.0 JSON schema (jsonschema crate)
JSON round-trip ScanReport serialize→deserialize identity for 1 000-finding report
Exit code 1 policy-violating scan exits 1; passing scan exits 0
Exit code 2 manifest parse error exits 2 (not 1)
SBOM diff correctness added/removed/changed correctly identified for fixture pair
Baseline delta new finding not in baseline → flagged; finding in both → suppressed from exit code
GitHub annotation format ::error lines parseable by GitHub Actions; no extra whitespace
SARIF line numbers findings reference manifest file + line where component declared
Table TTY detection piped output has no ANSI escape codes
Report generation latency ≤ 200 ms for 10 000 findings → JSON/SARIF on CI hardware

10.5 Conformance Fixtures

fixtures/report/sarif_expected.json golden SARIF output diff = 0 against emitted SARIF

10.6 Pitfalls

Pitfall Manifestation Root cause Debug signal Recovery
SARIF location missing GitHub shows finding with no file link SourceLocation not propagated from manifest parser SARIF physicalLocation is null Ensure scs-manifest stores SourceLocation; propagate through Finding
Exit code 0 on config error CI pipeline sees pass when scan failed silently error path returns Ok(()) with empty findings No findings + no error log is suspicious Treat ScsError returns as exit 2; never exit 0 on error
SARIF ruleId collision Two advisories map to same ruleId CVE and GHSA alias both emitted as separate rules with same id Duplicate ruleId in SARIF rules[] Deduplicate rules by canonical id; use primary id
Baseline stale purl Baseline finding purl differs from current (normalization change) Purl normalization changed between versions Delta shows all findings as "new" Pin purl normalization version; migration guide on version bump
ANSI in piped JSON JSON output contains ANSI codes is_terminal() returns true in some CI runners jq fails to parse Force --no-color in CI; check NO_COLOR env var
Large report OOM 100k findings JSON causes OOM to_string_pretty allocates full string in memory RSS spike visible in CI metrics Stream serialize with serde_json::to_writer + BufWriter
SBOM diff O(n²) Diff hangs on 100k-component SBOMs Inner loop naive comparison CPU 100%, no progress Sort-merge O(n log n); benchmark with scs-bench

10.7 Performance Concerns

  • JSON emission: use serde_json::to_writer with BufWriter<File> → avoids full-string allocation; 10k findings ≈ 5 MB, streamed.
  • SARIF rules dedup: build HashMap<AdvisoryId, SarifRule> once; O(1) lookup per finding.
  • Table rendering: comfy-table materializes full table in memory; cap at 1 000 displayed rows; --limit flag; print "... N more" for overflow.
  • SBOM diff: sort by PackageId key; two-pointer merge; O(n log n); no allocation per comparison.
  • Baseline load: deserialize once at startup; DashMap not needed (single-threaded report phase).
  • SARIF schema validation (test-only): jsonschema compile is expensive; cache compiled schema in OnceLock.
  • Annotation emission: write to stdout with BufWriter; flush once after all lines — avoid per-finding flush().

10.8 Edge Cases

  • Zero findings + policy pass → SARIF results: [], exit 0; not an error.
  • Findings with no manifest location → SARIF physicalLocation omitted (not null); GitHub handles gracefully.
  • --baseline points to report from different ecosystem subset → missing keys treated as "not in baseline" (conservative: flag as new).
  • SBOM diff with identical SBOMs → SbomDiff { added: [], removed: [], version_changed: [], unchanged_count: N }; exit 0.
  • Scan report timestamp timezone → always serialize as RFC 3339 UTC (Z suffix); no local timezone.
  • --format sarif + --baseline → delta findings only in SARIF results; full findings in separate JSON sidecar.
  • Report file write fails (disk full) → IoError → exit 2; partial file deleted.
  • Unicode in component names → SARIF message.text must be valid UTF-8; serde_json handles; table truncates at terminal width.
  • Severity Unknown (no CVSS) → SARIF level: "note"; table shows ? in severity column.

10.9 Failure Analysis

What breaks Detection Blast radius Containment Recovery Operator visibility
SARIF schema invalid CI code-scanning upload rejects file GitHub shows no results (silent) Validate in scs-bench CI fixture Fix emitter; schema test gates release Test ::warning in CI log if upload fails
Baseline file corrupt BaselineLoadError All findings appear "new" Log warning; continue without baseline Re-generate baseline from passing scan Structured warning in report header
Report write OOM Process killed Scan results lost --format json --output /dev/null to test Add --max-findings truncation flag RSS metric spike; OOM kill in CI log
Exit code masked by shell set -e pipeline catches 0 but misses 1 Policy failures silently pass Document set -o pipefail requirement CI template includes pipefail README CI integration guide
GitLab SAST schema mismatch GitLab rejects gl-sast-report.json No findings in MR widget GitLab validator in scs-bench Update emitter to current GitLab schema Error in GitLab pipeline artifacts

10.10 Operational Notes

  • Metrics: scs_report_emit_duration_ms{format} histogram; scs_report_findings_total{severity} gauge; scs_report_size_bytes{format} gauge.
  • Tracing spans: report::emit_sarif, report::emit_json, report::sbom_diff, report::baseline_delta.
  • Structured log fields: report_format, finding_count, baseline_path, output_path.
  • Versioning: ScanReport.scs_report_version = "1"; parsers check version field before deserializing; bump on breaking schema change.
  • CI templates: provide .github/workflows/scs-scan.yml and .gitlab-ci.yml examples in docs/; parameterize --policy, --format sarif, --baseline.
  • Artifact retention: baseline.json stored as CI artifact; next run downloads and passes as --baseline; recommend 30-day retention.
  • Report diffing: scs-cli diff-report a.json b.json shows finding delta between two JSON reports; useful for release comparison.
  • Rollback: report format is additive; old CI scripts ignore unknown JSON fields (serde deny_unknown_fields NOT set on ScanReport).
  • Compliance export: --format spdx emits SBOM-only; --format cyclonedx emits CycloneDX SBOM; these reuse scs-sbom emitters.

23. Phase 11 — Incremental + caching — content-addressed advisory + resolution cache, incremental re-scan on manifest delta, offline mode, determinism

11.1 Objectives

  • Content-addressed cache: cache advisory-feed index, resolved dependency graphs, and findings keyed by SHA-256(manifest_content || feed_snapshot_id); invalidate only on input change.
  • Incremental re-scan: detect manifest-file diffs since last scan; re-run only affected phases (resolve, match) for changed ecosystems; skip unchanged.
  • Offline mode: --offline flag; use cached advisory feed + resolved graph; fail if cache cold; no network I/O.
  • Determinism invariant: identical (manifest_files_hash, feed_snapshot_id, policy_hash) → byte-identical ScanReport JSON; enforced by cache-hit check.
  • Cache store: local disk (~/.cache/scs/ or $SCS_CACHE_DIR); content-addressed by Blake3 hash; LRU eviction with configurable max size; no external DB.
  • Scope: new crate scs-cache (thin); orchestration in scs-cli; no caching logic inside scs-core phase crates.
  • Constraints: cache is advisory-only; never cache provenance/Sigstore results (network freshness required); cache entries are signed with HMAC to detect tampering.

11.2 Detailed Implementation Steps

scs-cache/src/store.rs: CacheStore{dir:PathBuf, max_bytes:u64, hmac_key:[u8;32]} — content-addressed store design per B6; CacheKey=Blake3Hash (32 B), hex filename {key[0..2]}/{key[2..64]}.bin; get(key) reads file, verifies HMAC, zstd-decompresses, deserializes; put(key,entry) serializes, zstd-compresses (level 3), HMAC-signs, writes atomically (temp file + fsync + rename — no partial-write corruption); CacheEntry ∈ {ResolvedGraph(DependencyGraph), MatchedFindings(Vec<Finding>), FeedIndex(AdvisoryIndex), PolicyResult(PolicyResult)}; HMAC-SHA256 key from SCS_CACHE_KEY env or per-install random key at ~/.cache/scs/key (anti-poisoning); LRU eviction on put when total size > max_bytes, oldest mtime evicted first, in-process before write. scs-cache/src/manifest_hash.rs: hash_manifest_set(paths) -> Blake3Hash feeds each file's bytes into one Blake3 hasher in sorted-path order (deterministic); ManifestDelta{changed_ecosystems, unchanged_ecosystems}; compute_delta(prev_hashes, current_paths); prior hashes persisted at ~/.cache/scs/manifest_hashes.json, keyed by canonical project-root path. scs-cache/src/keys.rs: ResolutionKey::new(manifest_hash, ecosystem) = Blake3("resolve"‖hash‖ecosystem); MatchKey::new(resolution_hash, feed_snapshot_id) = Blake3("match"‖hash‖snapshot); FeedIndexKey::new(feed_snapshot_id) = Blake3("feed"‖snapshot); type-prefixing prevents cross-type key collision. Incremental orchestration (scs-cli/src/incremental.rs::run_incremental_scan): 1) hash all manifest files → manifest_hash; 2) per ecosystem, ResolutionKey hit skips scs-resolve, miss resolves + caches; 3) MatchKey hit skips scs-match, miss matches + caches; 4) always re-run scs-provenance + scs-threat (network-dependent, never cached); 5) always re-run scs-policy + scs-report (cheap; policy file may have changed); 6) persist manifest_hashes.json on success. Offline mode (scs-cli/src/offline.rs): --offline sets NetworkPolicy::Offline; scs-vulndb skips feed sync, loads FeedIndex from cache (miss → ScsError::OfflineCacheMiss); scs-provenance skips Rekor/Fulcio, emits ProvenanceResult::OfflineSkipped; every other phase reads cache only — no HTTP client instantiated. Determinism enforcement: ScanReport::canonical_json() uses serde_json with preserve_order disabled, sorted keys, BTreeMap only (no HashMap) anywhere in the serialization path; scs-bench determinism test runs one scan twice against a fixed feed snapshot and asserts canonical_json() equality. scs-cache/src/evict.rs: evict_lru(dir, max_bytes) — read_dir → (path,mtime,size) → sort by mtime → remove oldest until under budget; best-effort (a remove_file failure from concurrent access is skipped, not fatal); --cache-clear flag runs remove_dir_all then recreates the directory. Test harness: store.rs — put/get round-trip, HMAC tamper detection, concurrent same-key puts, eviction threshold; incremental.rs — modify one manifest, assert only its ecosystem re-resolves; determinism.rs — two identical scans → identical canonical_json(); offline.rs — cold cache + --offline → OfflineCacheMiss; atomic_write.rs — simulated kill mid-write leaves a cache entry absent or complete, never partial.

11.3 Deliverable

  • scs-cli scan on an unchanged project after a first scan completes in < 500 ms (cache hit for resolve + match).
  • scs-cli scan --offline succeeds with a warm cache; fails with a clear error on a cold cache.
  • scs-bench determinism test passes: two identical scans produce identical JSON reports.
  • Cache directory inspectable: ls -lh ~/.cache/scs/ shows content-addressed .bin files with sensible sizes.
  • --cache-clear wipes the cache; the next scan runs a full scan.

11.4 Acceptance Criteria

Check Target
Cache hit speedup second scan of unchanged 500-dep project ≤ 500 ms vs ≥ 5 s cold
Determinism two identical scans → byte-identical canonical_json() (enforced in CI)
HMAC tamper detection flip 1 byte in cached .bin file → CacheIntegrityError; scan re-runs
Offline cold cache --offline with no cache → ScsError::OfflineCacheMiss; exit code 2
Offline warm cache --offline with warm advisory cache → complete scan; 0 network calls
Incremental delta modify package.json; assert npm ecosystem re-resolved; cargo graph from cache
LRU eviction cache fills past max_bytes; eviction removes oldest; total stays under budget
Atomic write safety partial write simulation → no corrupt cache entries on read
Feed key isolation different feed_snapshot_id → different MatchKey; no stale match results
--cache-clear cache dir removed + recreated empty; next scan is full cold scan

Fixture Purpose Expected output
fixtures/cache/manifest_unchanged/ same manifests run twice second run: all ecosystems from cache
fixtures/cache/manifest_one_changed/ package.json modified, Cargo.lock unchanged npm re-resolved; cargo from cache
fixtures/cache/tampered_entry.bin HMAC-corrupted cache entry CacheIntegrityError; re-scan
fixtures/cache/offline_warm/ pre-populated cache dir --offline succeeds; 0 HTTP calls
fixtures/cache/offline_cold/ empty cache dir --offline → OfflineCacheMiss
fixtures/cache/determinism_a.json + determinism_b.json two golden scan reports canonical_json() byte-identical
fixtures/cache/eviction/ cache dir pre-filled to 99% of max_bytes one put triggers eviction; oldest removed
fixtures/cache/concurrent_put/ two processes put same key simultaneously one wins; both read valid entry afterward

11.6 Pitfalls

Pitfall Manifestation Root cause Debug signal Recovery
Stale resolution cached after dep update new dep added; cache hit returns old graph manifest hash covers only top-level manifest, not transitive lockfile findings miss new dep include lockfile in manifest hash input
Cache key collision across projects project A's graph returned for project B ResolutionKey does not include project root path wrong findings silently include canonical_project_root in key derivation
HMAC key loss all cache entries invalid after key rotation SCS_CACHE_KEY env changed or ~/.cache/scs/key deleted mass CacheIntegrityError on startup treat key loss as cold cache; warn once; re-populate
Provenance cached (wrong) stale Rekor inclusion served from cache someone accidentally caches ProvenanceResult verification always passes even for revoked certs provenance results explicitly excluded from cacheable types
Race on concurrent scans two scs-cli invocations write same cache key simultaneously TOCTOU between check-and-write duplicate .bin files or truncated entry atomic rename resolves; last writer wins; both valid
canonical_json non-determinism HashMap serialization order varies across runs serde_json serializes HashMap in insertion order (non-deterministic) determinism CI test flaps replace all HashMap in ScanReport with BTreeMap
Eviction removes in-use entries get after eviction returns None; scan re-runs fully eviction triggered between get and put in same process unexpected cache miss metric spike eviction only runs on put path; reads never evict
Large graph serialization DependencyGraph 50k nodes → 200 MB .bin file full graph serialized without pruning cache dir balloons cache only the minimal graph (adjacency list + versions, no redundant fields)

11.7 Performance Concerns

  • Blake3 hashing: ~1 GB/s; hashing 10 MB of manifest files ≈ 10 µs — negligible.
  • zstd compress/decompress: level 3 ≈ 500 MB/s; 10 MB graph → 2 MB compressed; decompression ≈ 4 ms.
  • Cache lookup: file stat + read; SSD ≈ 0.1 ms, HDD ≈ 5 ms; no in-memory index needed below 10k entries.
  • Atomic write: fsync is the bottleneck (≈ 1–5 ms on SSD), one per put; batch multiple entries per scan phase into a single put where possible.
  • Eviction scan: read_dir of a 10k-file cache dir ≈ 50 ms; runs only when size exceeds threshold; async-spawned so it never blocks the scan.
  • canonical_json serialization: BTreeMap sort adds O(k log k) per struct; ≈ 5 ms total for 10k findings.
  • Concurrent scans: file-level lock via fs2::FileExt::lock_exclusive on a .lock sidecar; held only during write, not during compress/serialize.
  • In-memory tier: DashMap<CacheKey, Arc<CacheEntry>> for within-process reuse across phases; 256 MB soft limit, evicted on put when exceeded.

11.8 Edge Cases

  • Cache dir on a read-only filesystem (e.g., container with read-only /home) → CacheStore::put fails silently; scan continues uncached; cache_write_errors_total increments.
  • SCS_CACHE_DIR=/dev/null → all reads return None, all writes succeed silently discarded — effectively disables caching.
  • Manifest file deleted between hash computation and scan phases → IoError; scan aborts exit 2; cache not written.
  • Feed snapshot id changes mid-scan (concurrent feed sync) → MatchKey computed with the new id; no stale match results served.
  • Cache entry > 1 GB (pathological monorepo) → eviction removes it immediately after write; that entry is always cold next scan; warn + suggest --no-cache.
  • Two scs-cli versions share a cache dir → CacheEntry deserialization version mismatch → DeserializeError → treated as a miss (forward-compatible via serde's deny_unknown_fields=false).
  • --offline with an unknown feed snapshot id → cached FeedIndex differs from current state → accept cached index; log the mismatch as a warning.
  • Symlinked manifests → canonicalize() before hashing; the symlink target is hashed, not the symlink path.

11.9 Failure Analysis

What breaks Detection Blast radius Containment Recovery Operator visibility
Cache dir corruption (disk error) IoError or deserialization failure on get All cache misses; full scans every run Graceful fallback to cold scan; log error --cache-clear; check disk health cache_errors_total metric; ERROR log with path
HMAC key file deleted All entries fail HMAC → mass misses Full scans until key regenerated Warn once; generate new key; treat all as cold New key auto-generated on next put WARN log "cache key missing; regenerated"
Cache poisoning attempt Tampered entry fails HMAC → miss Zero: tampered entry rejected HMAC enforcement No action needed; attacker gains nothing cache_integrity_violation_total counter
Incremental delta incorrect Changed manifest not detected Stale findings served Hash includes all manifests + lockfiles Re-run with --no-cache to verify incremental_skip_total{ecosystem} shows unexpected skip
Offline mode with expired feed --offline succeeds but feed is months old Findings miss new advisories Feed age check: FeedIndex.timestamp vs --max-feed-age Warn in report header; exit 3 if too stale feed_age_days metric; [STALE FEED] banner

11.10 Operational Notes

  • Metrics: scs_cache_hit_total{phase="resolve|match|feed"}, scs_cache_miss_total{phase}, scs_cache_write_errors_total, scs_cache_size_bytes (gauge), scs_cache_eviction_total, scs_incremental_skip_total{ecosystem}, scs_scan_duration_ms{mode="cold|incremental|offline"}.
  • Tracing spans: cache::get, cache::put, cache::evict, incremental::delta, offline::load_feed.
  • Structured log fields: cache_key, ecosystem, feed_snapshot_id, cache_hit:bool, entry_size_bytes.
  • Deployment: cache dir persists on developer machines and CI agents; CI cache key = hash(manifests) for artifact restore.
  • CI cache strategy: store ~/.cache/scs/ as a CI artifact keyed on hash(package.json, Cargo.lock, go.sum, ...); restore before scan, upload after.
  • Cache invalidation policy: "scs_cache_version":1 field per entry, bumped on breaking serialization change; old entries treated as misses.
  • Offline use case: air-gapped environments; pre-populate via scs-cli prefetch --output cache.tar.zst, distribute, restore to SCS_CACHE_DIR.
  • Security: HMAC key at ~/.cache/scs/key, mode 0600; warn if permissions are broader; HMAC prevents cache poisoning from other local users.
  • Compatibility: scs-cache is internal, no public API guarantee; cache entries version independently of ScanReport schema.
  • Monitoring hook: --cache-stats prints hit/miss/size/eviction counters to stderr; used to tune max_bytes in CI.

24. Phase 12 — Observability — scan metrics (components, findings by severity, feed freshness, FP tracking), tracing, structured logs, audit log

12.1 Objectives

  • Instrument every crate with tracing spans + structured fields; no bare println!/eprintln! after Phase 12.
  • Expose Prometheus-compatible metrics via a /metrics HTTP endpoint (scrape-able by CI runners and k8s sidecars).
  • Emit a structured JSON audit log: one record per scan (inputs, feed snapshot id, findings count, gate decision, duration).
  • Track false-positive (FP) rate longitudinally: Finding.vex_status transitions feed a per-advisory FP counter.
  • All metrics/logs scoped to a scan_id (UUIDv7) propagated through every crate via tracing::Span context.
  • Constraints: no metrics library requiring an async runtime in scs-core (metrics crate push-based counters + tracing-subscriber only); observability overhead budget ≤2% wall-clock regression on the large-repo bench (Phase 13 gate).

12.2 Detailed Implementation Steps

Crate instrumentation (ADR-017 tracing, ADR-018 metrics):

Crate Change
scs-core ScanId(Uuid) newtype + ScanContext{scan_id,feed_snapshot_id,started_at} passed via Arc<ScanContext> through the pipeline; ScanMetrics struct: components_total, findings_by_severity:EnumMap<Severity,u64>, feed_age_secs, parse_errors, match_misses, fp_suppressed.
scs-cli tracing_subscriber init with EnvFilter (default info), optional JSON formatter via --log-format=json; spawns /metrics HTTP server (tiny_http), registers metrics_exporter_prometheus global recorder at startup.
scs-manifest #[tracing::instrument(skip(raw))] on each parser entry; records ecosystem, file_path, component_count.
scs-resolve Span resolve_graph with fields root_count, edge_count, cycle_count; histogram!("scs.resolve.depth_max").
scs-vulndb counter!("scs.vulndb.feed_sync_total","feed"=>feed_name); gauge!("scs.vulndb.feed_age_secs",age_f64); warn-log if feed age > 24h.
scs-match Per-match tracing::event!(Level::DEBUG,purl,advisory_id,verdict); counter!("scs.match.findings_total","severity"=>...).
scs-provenance counter!("scs.provenance.verify_ok_total") / verify_fail_total{reason}; fail-closed path emits Level::ERROR.
scs-threat counter!("scs.threat.heuristic_total","kind"=>typosquatting|confusion|script); Level::WARN per triggered heuristic.
scs-policy counter!("scs.policy.gate_total","result"=>pass|fail); audit record includes waiver IDs consumed.
scs-report Writes audit entry to audit.jsonl (append, rotated by scan_id prefix); emits Level::INFO summary line.

Audit log record (NDJSON, one line/scan):

{ "scan_id":"<uuid>", "feed_snapshot_id":"<sha256>", "started_at":"<rfc3339>", "duration_ms":4210,
  "ecosystems":["npm","cargo"], "components_total":842, "direct":31, "transitive":811,
  "findings":{"critical":1,"high":3,"medium":7,"low":12}, "fp_suppressed":2, "gate":"fail",
  "waivers_applied":["w-001"], "parse_errors":0, "feed_age_secs":3610 }

FP tracking: Finding.vex_status:Option<VexStatus> (NotAffected/Mitigated/WillNotFix/Fixed); policy crate increments scs.fp_suppressed_total when VEX suppresses a finding; audit record carries fp_suppressed count so a periodic cron can diff logs per advisory for sustained FP patterns; no ML inference in scope — FP detection is advisory-id-keyed counter export only. tracing span nesting (parent scan{scan_id} span; children inherit scan_id via context): scan{scan_id} -> manifest::parse{ecosystem} -> resolve::graph -> vulndb::match -> policy::gate. Structured log levels: ERROR — provenance fail-closed, policy gate=fail, parse error on required manifest; WARN — feed age >24h, typosquatting hit, dependency confusion hit, cycle detected; INFO — scan start/finish summary, gate decision; DEBUG — per-component match verdicts, per-advisory lookup; TRACE — inner tokenizer/lexer steps (disabled by default).

12.3 Deliverable

  • scs-cli --metrics-port 9090 starts a Prometheus scrape endpoint; metrics visible via curl localhost:9090/metrics.
  • audit.jsonl appended per scan; machine-parseable; fields stable across versions (additive-only schema).
  • RUST_LOG=scs=debug produces per-component trace; RUST_LOG=scs=info produces a clean CI summary.
  • tracing-forest/tracing-chrome can attach to produce flamegraph-style span timelines.
  • All Phase-0–11 tests pass with the observability layer enabled (no regression).

12.4 Acceptance Criteria

Check Threshold
Wall-clock regression (large-repo bench, Phase 13) ≤ 2 % vs no-metrics baseline
scs.findings_total{severity="critical"} counter accuracy Matches Finding count in report exactly (unit test)
Audit log round-trip parse serde_json::from_str succeeds on every emitted record; no missing required fields
Feed-age gauge updates Gauge reflects actual mtime delta ± 1 s (integration test)
FP suppression counter Equals vex_not_affected_count in report (property test, 1000 iterations)
Span propagation scan_id field present on every child span (tracing subscriber capture test)
Metrics endpoint availability HTTP 200 within 50 ms of CLI start (integration test)
Log level filtering RUST_LOG=error suppresses all INFO/DEBUG lines (no stray output test)

fixtures/obs/scan_with_critical.json Known critical finding → verify counter increments scs.findings_total{severity="critical"} 1 in /metrics
fixtures/obs/stale_feed.sqlite Feed mtime = 48 h ago → warn-log WARN feed_age_secs=172800 ... in structured log

12.6 Pitfalls

Pitfall Manifestation Root Cause Debug Signal Recovery
Metric counter double-increment findings count 2× actual Span entered twice (e.g., retry loop) Add assert_eq in test; trace counter! call sites Counter value > Finding vec len Guard increment with once_cell per scan_id
scan_id dropped at async boundary Child spans missing scan_id field tracing::Span::enter dropped before await tracing-futures Instrument wrapper missing Span without scan_id in collector Wrap async blocks with .instrument(span)
Audit file descriptor leak FD exhaustion on high-frequency CI File::open not closed after append lsof / ulimit check OS FD count metric Use BufWriter + explicit drop; open-append-close per scan
Metrics HTTP port conflict /metrics bind fails silently Port 9090 in use ss -tlnp ERROR bind failed log Configurable port; --no-metrics flag to disable
Feed-age gauge clock skew Gauge reports negative age System clock adjusted between mtime read and now chrono::Utc::now() vs SystemTime::now() Negative gauge value Use SystemTime; clamp to 0 if negative
Log noise in CI INFO lines drown test output Default filter too permissive RUST_LOG=error cargo test Line count in CI log Default filter warn in test harness; override in CI env
Audit JSONL corrupt on crash Partial line appended Process killed mid-write `tail -1 audit.jsonl jq .` JSON parse error

12.7 Performance Concerns

  • counter!/gauge! macros use thread-local sharding (metrics crate, ≤10 ns/call); avoid per-component String allocation in labels — use &'static str for severity/ecosystem values.
  • /metrics scrape serializes all counters; high label cardinality (per-advisory) can bloat the response — cap advisory-keyed metrics, aggregate by severity only.
  • JSON audit log: serde_json::to_writer + BufWriter<File>, avoiding intermediate String allocation.
  • tracing span creation: prefer the instrument macro over manual Span::new to avoid per-event allocations.
  • Disable TRACE-level spans behind cfg!(debug_assertions); metrics HTTP server is single-threaded tiny_http (scrape interval ~15s — no concurrency concern).

12.8 Edge Cases

  • scan_id UUIDv7 collision probability is negligible (monotonic+random, no dedup needed); audit file missing directory → scs-report must create_dir_all before first append.
  • Scan duration overflow: u64 ms overflows at ~585M years; metrics counter wrap: u64 max ~1.8×10^19 — neither is a practical risk per process lifetime.
  • Zero-component scan (empty lockfile): all counters at 0; audit record valid; gate = pass by policy default.
  • Concurrent scans to the same audit file: each appends one line; O_APPEND is atomic for lines ≤ PIPE_BUF on POSIX.
  • Feed snapshot ID changes mid-scan: ID captured at scan start; subsequent vulndb reads use the same snapshot — no mid-scan drift.

12.9 Failure Analysis

Failure Detection Blast Radius Containment Recovery
Metrics exporter panics Process exits; CI scan fails Entire scan lost Catch panic in metrics thread; scan continues without metrics --no-metrics fallback flag
Audit JSONL directory unwritable std::io::Error on open Audit lost for scan; scan result valid Log ERROR; continue scan; surface in exit code Fix permissions; replay from SARIF
Tracing subscriber init failure Panic at startup No scan output at all tracing::subscriber::set_global_default returns Err — handle gracefully, fall back to stderr Diagnose subscriber conflict (two set_global_default calls)
Feed age gauge stuck (vulndb offline) Gauge shows stale value indefinitely Misleads operators on freshness Emit WARN on each scan if age > threshold Manual feed refresh; alert on gauge threshold in Prometheus
Log line truncation (stdout buffer full) Missing INFO lines in CI Incomplete audit trail Flush after each scan summary line Increase CI log buffer; write to file in addition to stdout

12.10 Operational Notes

  • Metrics: Prometheus endpoint at --metrics-port (default 9090); key dashboards: scs.findings_total by severity, scs.vulndb.feed_age_secs (alert >86400), scs.provenance.verify_fail_total (alert >0), scs.policy.gate_total{result="fail"}.
  • Tracing: compatible with OpenTelemetry via tracing-opentelemetry bridge; OTLP export to Jaeger/Tempo.
  • Audit log & rotation: append-only audit.jsonl, rotated by date in the deployment wrapper, ingested into SIEM/BigQuery for trend analysis; external rotation (logrotate, vector) — scanner does not self-rotate; --audit-log=/dev/stdout for ephemeral CI.
  • Rollback: metrics/logging are additive; SCS_NO_METRICS=1 disables them without affecting correctness.
  • Schema stability: audit record fields are additive-only; consumers opt into #[serde(deny_unknown_fields)], not default.
  • Compatibility: tracing 0.1.x + metrics 0.22.x, both MSRV ≥1.75 — compatible with MSRV 1.78.

25. Phase 13 — Conformance, differential testing, fuzzing, benchmarks — vs OSV-Scanner/Grype on fixtures; fuzz manifest parsers; large-dependency-tree benches

13.1 Objectives

  • Differential conformance: SCS findings on shared fixtures must not have false-negatives vs OSV-Scanner and Grype (same advisory set, same lockfile).
  • Fuzz all manifest/lockfile parsers (scs-manifest) with cargo-fuzz (libFuzzer) + afl.rs targets; zero panic/UB/OOB in corpus of 10 M inputs.
  • Benchmark scs-resolve + scs-match on synthetic large trees (1 k, 10 k, 100 k nodes); establish regression baselines.
  • Conformance harness lives in scs-bench; golden files checked into fixtures/conformance/.
  • Constraint: differential runner must work offline (pre-captured OSV-Scanner/Grype JSON outputs; no live network in CI).
  • No new public API surface from this phase; all additions are #[cfg(test)] or scs-bench binary.

13.2 Detailed Implementation Steps

Differential conformance (scs-bench/src/diff.rs, vs R1 OSV-Scanner / R2 Grype): fixture corpus fixtures/conformance/{ecosystem}/ holds lockfile + expected osv_scanner_output.json+grype_output.json+scs_expected.json; normalize_finding(f:&RawFinding) -> NormalizedFinding{advisory_id:canonical_cve_or_ghsa, purl, severity_band} strips tool-specific fields; comparison asserts every OSV-Scanner/Grype normalized finding exists in SCS output (zero FN allowed) — SCS may report MORE findings (broader feed) which is not a failure, only a logged count delta; CVE↔GHSA↔OSV aliasing resolved via scs-vulndb's alias table before comparison; scs-bench diff --fixture fixtures/conformance/npm/lodash/ --tool osv-scanner prints a diff table, exits non-zero on FN. Fuzz targets (fuzz/fuzz_targets/, ADR-020 cargo-fuzz/libFuzzer): fuzz_npm_lockfile(scs-manifest::npm::parse_package_lock, seeds: 100+ real package-lock.json); fuzz_cargo_lock(scs-manifest::cargo::parse_cargo_lock, seeds: Cargo.lock from top-500 crates.io); fuzz_pypi_requirements(scs-manifest::pypi::parse_requirements, seeds: requirements.txt variants); fuzz_go_sum(scs-manifest::go::parse_go_sum, seeds: go.sum samples); fuzz_maven_pom(scs-manifest::maven::parse_pom, seeds: pom.xml samples); fuzz_spdx_sbom(scs-sbom::spdx::parse, seeds: SPDX TV+JSON samples); fuzz_cyclonedx_sbom(scs-sbom::cyclonedx::parse, seeds: CycloneDX JSON samples); fuzz_osv_advisory(scs-vulndb::osv::parse_advisory, seeds: OSV JSON from feed). Fuzz harness pattern: libfuzzer_sys::fuzz_target!(|data:&[u8]| { let _ = scs_manifest::npm::parse_package_lock(data); }) — must not panic; ASan+UBSan enabled via [profile.fuzz] in the workspace Cargo.toml; corpus at fuzz/corpus/{target}/, minimized corpus committed; CI runs each target 60s (-max_total_time=60), nightly runs 30 min each. Benchmarks (scs-bench/src/benches/): bench_resolve_1k(scs-resolve, synthetic 1k-node DAG max-depth 12, p50/p99 wall-clock); bench_resolve_10k(scs-resolve, synthetic 10k-node DAG, p50/p99); bench_resolve_100k(scs-resolve, synthetic 100k-node DAG stress, p50/p99 + peak RSS); bench_match_10k(scs-match, 10k components × full OSV feed snapshot, findings/sec throughput); bench_parse_npm_large(scs-manifest, package-lock.json 5MB real-world, parse time ms); bench_parse_cargo_large(scs-manifest, Cargo.lock 3MB, parse time ms); bench_sbom_roundtrip(scs-sbom, 10k-component SPDX, emit+parse time ms); bench_policy_10k(scs-policy, 10k findings + complex policy, gate decision time ms). Harness: criterion crate, criterion::black_box guards, HTML report via criterion --output-format bencher in CI; thresholds enforced by scs-bench/scripts/check_thresholds.sh comparing target/criterion/ JSON. Synthetic graph generator (scs-bench/src/gen.rs::gen_dag(nodes,max_depth,seed) -> DependencyGraph): deterministic via rand::SeedableRng(ChaCha8), reproducible across runs; no cycles (topological layer assignment); realistic fan-out (Zipf-distributed degree). Regression baselines: fixtures/bench_baselines.json keyed by benchmark name → {p50_ms,p99_ms,rss_mb}; CI fails if measured p99 > baseline×1.20 (20% budget); update via scs-bench update-baseline + commit. Differential fixture layout (fixtures/conformance/{ecosystem}/{package-version}/, e.g. npm/lodash-4.17.20/, cargo/tokio-1.28/, pypi/django-3.2/): each dir holds the input lockfile/manifest + pre-captured osv_scanner_output.json+grype_output.json+scs_expected.json (expected NormalizedFinding list); sibling metadata.json pins osv_scanner_version, grype_version, feed_snapshot_date, advisory_ids for that fixture (e.g. {"osv_scanner_version":"1.7.4","grype_version":"0.78.0","feed_snapshot_date":"2025-01-15","advisory_ids":["CVE-2021-23337","GHSA-..."]}).

13.3 Deliverable

  • cargo fuzz run fuzz_npm_lockfile -- -max_total_time=60 completes with no crash/panic on CI.
  • scs-bench diff --all-fixtures exits 0 (no false-negatives vs pinned OSV-Scanner/Grype outputs).
  • cargo bench produces criterion HTML + JSON; check_thresholds.sh exits 0 within baselines.
  • Minimized fuzz corpora committed; new fuzz finds tracked as issues.
  • Benchmark history graphed in CI artifact; 20 % regression gate enforced.

13.4 Acceptance Criteria

Check Threshold
Differential FN rate vs OSV-Scanner 0 false-negatives on all conformance fixtures
Differential FN rate vs Grype 0 false-negatives on all conformance fixtures
Fuzz: panic/UB/OOB in 10 M inputs 0 crashes across all 8 targets
bench_resolve_10k p99 ≤ 500 ms
bench_resolve_100k p99 ≤ 8 s
bench_match_10k throughput ≥ 5 k findings/sec
bench_parse_npm_large (5 MB) ≤ 200 ms
Peak RSS bench_resolve_100k ≤ 512 MB
CI fuzz run (60 s each) 0 crashes in every merge CI run
Criterion regression gate p99 ≤ baseline × 1.20 for all benchmarks

13.5 Conformance Fixtures

Fixture Purpose Expected Output Regression / Malformed Cases
npm/lodash-4.17.20/ CVE-2021-23337 prototype pollution — known CVE FN=0 vs Grype+OSV Replay with updated feed snapshot → still found
npm/minimist-vuln/ CVE-2020-7598 — transitive-only dep FN=0; dep-path includes minimist Lockfile with extra whitespace → parse succeeds
cargo/time-0.1/ RUSTSEC-2020-0071 — ecosystem-specific advisory FN=0 vs OSV (Rust ecosystem) Cargo.lock with duplicate entries → deduplicated
pypi/django-3.2/ CVE-2021-35042 SQL injection FN=0 requirements.txt with VCS deps → skipped gracefully
go/runc-old/ CVE-2019-5736 container escape FN=0 go.sum with hash mismatch → parse error surfaced
maven/log4j-2.14/ CVE-2021-44228 log4shell FN=0; critical severity pom.xml with broken XML → parse error, not panic
sbom/cyclonedx-roundtrip/ 500-component CycloneDX → emit → parse All components preserved; no relationship loss CycloneDX with unknown extensions → unknown fields ignored
fuzz/corpus/npm/ Minimized crash-reproducing inputs from prior runs Parser returns Err, no panic Empty file, NUL bytes, max-len UTF-8, truncated JSON

13.6 Pitfalls

Pitfall Manifestation Root Cause Debug Signal Recovery
Advisory alias mismatch in diff FN reported when SCS uses CVE, Grype uses GHSA Alias table incomplete Inspect NormalizedFinding.advisory_id vs Grype output FN count > 0 for known fixture Expand alias table; add OSV alias resolution
Fuzz corpus explosion Corpus dir grows to GB; CI slow Corpus minimization not run cargo fuzz cmin not in CI pipeline Corpus dir size metric Add cargo fuzz cmin step; commit minimized corpus only
