  src/sync/nvd.rs       — NVD CVE 2.0 API ingestion, CPE->purl mapping
  src/sync/ghsa.rs      — GitHub Advisory GraphQL ingestion
  src/index.rs          — per-ecosystem version-range index (B-tree keyed by PackageId)
  src/normalize.rs      — advisory normalization to internal Advisory struct
  src/snapshot.rs       — feed snapshot metadata (source, etag, timestamp, record count)
  tests/vulndb_test.rs  — ingest fixtures, query correctness, snapshot idempotency

scs-match/
  src/lib.rs            — match_all(): DependencyGraph x VulnDb -> Vec<Finding>
  src/range.rs          — version-range satisfiability per ecosystem (half-open semantics)
  src/semver_range.rs   — SemVer range evaluation (Cargo/npm dialect differences)
  src/pep440_range.rs   — PEP 440 specifier evaluation (epochs, pre-releases, ~=)
  src/maven_range.rs    — Maven version range evaluation ([1.0,2.0) syntax)
  src/correlation.rs    — CVE<->GHSA<->OSV alias dedup, canonical Finding ID
  src/path.rs           — dep-path extraction from DependencyGraph for each Finding
  src/withdrawn.rs      — withdrawn/disputed advisory suppression/flagging
  tests/match_test.rs   — boundary conditions, off-by-one, alias correlation, withdrawn

Security, policy and reporting crates (scs-provenance through scs-report):

scs-provenance/
  src/lib.rs            — verify(): Component -> Provenance
  src/sigstore.rs       — cosign signature verification, Fulcio cert chain validation
  src/rekor.rs          — Rekor inclusion proof verification (Merkle path)
  src/intoto.rs         — in-toto DSSE envelope parse + layout/link policy eval
  src/slsa.rs           — SLSA level inference from attestation contents
  src/hash_pin.rs       — hash-pinning verification (manifest declared vs actual)
  src/fail_closed.rs    — verification error -> Finding (never silently passing)
  tests/provenance_test.rs — fixture attestations, bad signatures, missing Rekor proofs

scs-threat/
  src/lib.rs            — analyze(): Vec<Component> -> Vec<Finding> (ThreatCategory)
  src/typosquat.rs      — edit-distance + phonetic similarity vs popular-package corpus
  src/dep_confusion.rs  — registry-origin check, private-namespace vs public match
  src/install_script.rs — npm preinstall/postinstall, setup.py, build.rs static scan
  src/entropy.rs        — Shannon entropy + obfuscation heuristics in script bodies
  src/signals.rs        — maintainer history, package age, download-velocity anomaly
  src/corpus.rs         — popular-package name corpus loader + trie index
  tests/threat_test.rs  — typosquat fixtures, dep-confusion scenarios, malicious scripts

scs-policy/
  src/lib.rs            — evaluate(): Vec<Finding> + Policy -> GateDecision
  src/config.rs         — Policy TOML schema (thresholds, license lists, waivers, EPSS)
  src/severity.rs       — severity-threshold gating logic per Finding
  src/license.rs        — SPDX expression allow/deny eval, copyleft detection
  src/vex.rs            — VEX (OpenVEX/CSAF) ingestion + Finding suppression
  src/waiver.rs         — waiver/ignore file parse, expiry enforcement
  src/gate.rs           — GateDecision enum (Pass/Fail/Warn), exit-code mapping
  tests/policy_test.rs  — threshold edge cases, VEX suppression, waiver expiry

scs-report/
  src/lib.rs            — report(): Vec<Finding> + ReportConfig -> Output
  src/sarif.rs          — SARIF 2.1.0 JSON serialization
  src/json.rs           — structured JSON summary output
  src/table.rs          — terminal table (comfy-table), color severity
  src/sbom_diff.rs      — SBOM diff narrative (added/removed/changed)
  src/annotation.rs     — GitHub/GitLab PR annotation format helpers
  src/exit_code.rs      — exit-code enum (0=pass, 1=policy-fail, 2=scan-error)
  tests/report_test.rs  — SARIF schema validation, JSON round-trip, table smoke

CLI, benchmarks, and workspace root files (scs-cli, scs-bench, CI configs):

scs-cli/
  src/main.rs           — clap entry point, subcommand dispatch
  src/cmd/scan.rs       — `scs scan` subcommand (orchestrates all crates)
  src/cmd/sbom.rs       — `scs sbom` emit/diff subcommand
  src/cmd/vulndb.rs     — `scs vulndb sync/query` subcommand
  src/cmd/verify.rs     — `scs verify` provenance-only subcommand
  src/cmd/policy.rs     — `scs policy check` dry-run subcommand
  src/config.rs         — config file discovery + merge (workspace > project > env)
  src/progress.rs       — indicatif progress bars for long-running scan stages
  src/telemetry.rs      — tracing subscriber init, OTLP exporter setup
  tests/cli_test.rs     — integration tests via assert_cmd + fixture workspaces

scs-bench/
  src/lib.rs            — bench harness entry
  src/fixtures.rs       — fixture manifest/lockfile sets (small/medium/large)
  src/differential.rs   — differential scan (old vs new advisory snapshot) correctness
  src/perf.rs           — criterion benchmarks: parse, resolve, match throughput
  benches/scan.rs       — end-to-end scan benchmark (criterion)
  data/                 — fixture lockfiles (real-world, anonymized)
  data/advisories/      — frozen advisory snapshots for determinism tests

.github/
  workflows/ci.yml      — CI: fmt, clippy, test, SARIF upload
  workflows/release.yml — release: cross-compile, SBOM generation, sign
  SECURITY.md           — vuln disclosure policy
  CODEOWNERS            — per-crate ownership

Cargo.toml              — workspace root, edition 2021, MSRV 1.78, shared deps
Cargo.lock              — workspace lockfile (committed)
deny.toml               — cargo-deny: license + advisory + duplicate checks
.cargo/config.toml      — target aliases, linker flags, RUSTFLAGS
policy.example.toml     — annotated policy template for users
scs.example.toml        — annotated scanner config template

Appendix B — Milestone Summary

Phase Deliverable Duration Key Risks Exit Criteria
0 Workspace scaffold, scs-core types, CI skeleton, deny.toml 1 week Purl/Version abstraction leaks ecosystem assumptions All core types compile; CI green; MSRV 1.78 enforced
1 scs-manifest: npm + Cargo parsers, purl gen, hash extraction 1.5 weeks Cargo workspace graphs, lockfile v3 quirks Parse all fixture lockfiles; round-trip purl; 0 panics on malformed input
2 scs-manifest: PyPI + Go + Maven + Gem parsers 1.5 weeks PEP 440 pre-release edge cases, Maven property interpolation Full fixture coverage per ecosystem; reconcile lockfile-vs-manifest
3 scs-resolve: graph construction, constraint solve, cycle detection, scope propagation 2 weeks pubgrub edge cases, large monorepo graphs (>10k nodes) Resolve all fixture workspaces; cycle detection stable; dev-scope prune correct
4 scs-vulndb: OSV + GHSA + NVD ingestion, snapshot metadata, persistent index 2 weeks NVD rate limits, CPE->purl mapping fidelity Ingest live OSV+GHSA; ETag-incremental sync; snapshot reproducible
5 scs-match: version-range satisfiability (SemVer/PEP440/Maven), alias correlation, path extraction 2 weeks Off-by-one on range bounds, dialect differences (npm vs Cargo semver) 0 false-negatives on test advisory set; boundary unit tests pass
6 scs-sbom: SPDX + CycloneDX emit/parse, round-trip fuzz, diff 1 week Relationship graph fidelity, CycloneDX 1.6 schema gaps Round-trip fuzz 10k iterations 0 failures; diff output correct
7 scs-provenance: Sigstore/cosign verify, Rekor inclusion, in-toto DSSE, SLSA level, hash-pin 2 weeks Rekor API availability, Fulcio cert expiry, in-toto Layout complexity Verify real cosign-signed artifacts; fail-closed on tampered sig
8 scs-threat: typosquatting, dep-confusion, install-script scan, entropy heuristics 1.5 weeks False-positive rate on legitimate packages, corpus coverage Detect all known test-case attacks; FP rate <2% on popular-package corpus
9 scs-policy: severity+EPSS thresholds, license eval, VEX suppression, waivers, gate 1 week VEX format fragmentation (OpenVEX vs CSAF), waiver expiry edge cases Policy gate produces correct pass/fail on fixture finding sets
10 scs-report: SARIF, JSON, table, SBOM diff, exit codes, CI annotation 1 week SARIF schema validation failures in GitHub UI, color/width in CI SARIF validates against schema; exit codes correct; CI annotation renders
11 scs-cli: all subcommands, config merge, progress, telemetry, OTLP 1 week Config precedence bugs, tracing overhead at high verbosity CLI integration tests pass; OTLP spans emitted; --help complete
12 scs-bench: fixtures, differential correctness, criterion benchmarks, performance tuning 1.5 weeks Benchmark variance, regression detection threshold calibration Scan <10s for 5k-node graph; no regressions vs baseline; differential 0 missed findings
13 Hardening: fuzz campaign (cargo-fuzz), supply-chain self-scan, docs, release pipeline, SBOM sign 2 weeks Fuzzer-found panics in parsers, self-scan policy failures 48h fuzz 0 crashes; self-scan passes own policy; signed release SBOM published
